- 14.09.26 11:25 [E-128] [parent: E-127] [T-022] [agent: claude-opus-5] [op: open-t022] DEC: T-022 Elemental Click VFX OPENED (T-021 closed DONE at E-127; user instruction "davai dalshe"). SCOPE: Air/Fire/Water/Earth as ClickStyle 7..10 on the accepted T-017 click architecture; the seven existing click styles are not retuned. USER DECISION captured this run: elemental identity is MOTION/SHAPE **plus** an elemental hue bias blended over the user's Click color -- option "elemental tint over your color", NOT motion-only and NOT fixed colors that ignore the Click color setting. CONSEQUENCE: new bounded ClickConfig field element_tint (0..1, default 0.65) and schema 5 -> 6 migration (a schema-5 file lacks the key and keeps the default; no pre-existing field is touched); the click style enum range check in config_storage widens from 0..6 to 0..10 with invalid values still recovering to Ring. MOTION CONTRACTS: Air tangential swirl (angle advances with progress) + fast thin wide ring, no fill; Fire buoyant embers rising with lateral wobble, shrinking, deterministic flicker, tint cooling with age, no ring; Water up to 3 concentric ripple rings born at staggered progress phases, outer-first; Earth thick low dust ring to ~60% radius + chunky debris thrown outward under gravity, ending BELOW the anchor. All pure functions of progress and particle index (stateless, frame-cadence independent), reusing the existing sink primitives add_bubble/add_particle -- no new renderer primitive, no shaders, no particle editor. Bounds unchanged: particle_amount 0..24 per click, <=3 rings, kMaxActiveBubbles 128. PLAN: config layer -> effect math -> Settings selector + Element tint row -> per-element motion regressions + tint blend + schema tests -> clean Release+Debug -> full CTest -> fresh portable package -> isolated deployed smoke -> user visual gate. T-021 evidence and artifacts are NOT touched by this ticket.

- 14.09.26 11:40 [E-129] [parent: E-128] [T-022] [agent: claude-opus-5] [op: t022-build-complete] RUN: T-022 Elemental Click VFX BUILT AND PACKAGED; T-022 BUILD -> VERIFY. CODE: ClickStyle gains Air(7) Fire(8) Water(9) Earth(10); ClickConfig gains element_tint (0..1, default 0.65) with kMinElementTint/kMaxElementTint bounds, validated() clamping and invalid-style recovery widened to the new values; is_elemental_click_style() lives next to the enum in click_config.h so the config layer, the effect and the Settings UI share ONE definition and the UI never depends on the effect class; AppConfig::kCurrentSchemaVersion 5 -> 6 with the documented 'a schema-5 file simply lacks the key' migration; config_storage serializes element_tint and widens the style range check 0..6 -> 0..10. EFFECT (pure, stateless, no new sink primitive, no shaders): hash01() exposes the existing Spark Burst mixer; element_target()/elemental_color() blend the user Click color toward the element hue by element_tint (0 = exactly the user colour, 1 = exactly the target), with Fire additionally COOLING as its embers age; elemental_particle_count() gives each family its own budget (Air/Fire = amount, Water = amount/3 accent droplets, Earth = about half with a floor of 3, and 0 always means 0); elemental_particle() carries the four motion signatures -- Air turns (angle advances 2.4 rad per unit progress while drifting out monotonically), Fire rises (y strictly decreases, lateral wobble only, shrinking, deterministic flicker), Water arcs droplets under its rings, Earth is thrown outward and FALLS below the anchor under gravity with chunky barely-shrinking debris; water_ripple() emits up to 3 concentric rings born at staggered progress phases 0.26 apart, each with its own life so the leading ring is the largest and faintest; draw() emits the element ring/flash FIRST and its particles on top. SETTINGS: four more selector buttons in the click STYLE grid (eleven total, T-020 selector contract, never a combo box) plus an Element tint row shown only for the four elements; Fire additionally hides Outline because it draws no outlined ring. DEFECT FOUND AND REPAIRED DURING BUILD: the extra options row pushed the Click page past the scroll viewport, and from there hiding the Outline row re-flowed ANIMATION and moved its anchor by 2 px (caught by click_style_options_region_reserves_space at click_style_spark_burst); Outline and Fill opacity now sit in a bounded fixed-height host click_param_options, the same device click_style_options already used, so no section height depends on the selected style. Two config tests that hard-coded 'schema_version 5' were rewritten to assert kCurrentSchemaVersion, so a future schema bump cannot turn them into stale constant checks. EVIDENCE: clean Release+Debug /W4 /WX 0 errors 0 warnings (build_t022_clean); CTest Release 15/15 PASS, Debug 15/15 PASS with protrail_input_dispatch EXCLUDED (pointer-stealing SendInput suite; the full 16/16 run is the remaining gate and needs explicit user consent); click 1822 checks 0 failures; config 30/30; GUI 82/82; the anchor sweep now covers all eleven styles.
